Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a mol_bio algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 714. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 3/1.
